# Case comparison: CI #68871 — GB300-4_GPUs-PyTorch-Post-Merge-3 (part 2)

[Back to report](../REPORT.en.md) · [All parts](INDEX.md)

Each row is an exact `(JUnit classname, test name)` match. This includes outer pytest wrappers and inner tests; their durations overlap and must not be summed. Each run's repeated identity is kept visible; deltas are calculated only for a unique passing on record and at least one unique passing eligible off record. Off observations whose stage was not a successful baseline are displayed but excluded from the median. Failed, skipped, timeout, baseline-only and new cases are retained. A dash means missing or not comparable, not zero. These are cross-commit observations and do not establish causality.
